<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-50060694" data-id="50060694" data-tags="Programming|Security|Code" data-score="320" data-comments="193" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50060694" data-time="1791829744">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/48195" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex CLI installs a persistent, self-updating background daemon by default</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 默认安装一个持久且自更新的后台守护进程</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Codex CLI now ships with a background daemon that persists and self-updates by default, raising concerns about user consent, security, and potential persistence. Critics warn this can create a form of &#x27;auto-update&#x27; risk and complicate auditing. This change could influence how developers package CLIs and how users trust automation.</span><span class='zh'>Codex CLI 现默认随附一个持续运行并自更新的后台守护进程，引发对用户同意、安全性及长期持久化的担忧。有人担心这类自动更新会带来可追溯性和审计难题。这一变动可能影响开发者在打包 CLI 时的做法以及用户对自动化工具的信任。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://z.ai/blog/glm-legal" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM-Legal</span><span class='zh'>GLM 法律问题</span></a><span class="dom">z.ai</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post outlines legal considerations for GLMs, including licensing, data rights, and compliance. It discusses risk management for developers and organizations deploying GLMs.</span><span class='zh'>本文概述了 GLM 的法律考量，包括许可、数据权利和合规性，讨论了在部署 GLM 时开发者和组织应如何进行风险管理。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/10/ai-coding-agents-generate-more-code-but-not-more-software/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-2264559974-1152x648.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='arstechnica.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/ai/2026/10/ai-coding-agents-generate-more-code-but-not-more-software/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI coding agents generate more code, but not more software</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理生成更多代码，但并未带来更多软件</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI coding agents can generate more code, but this doesn&#x27;t translate into more usable software due to quality and integration hurdles. The article underscores the need for better tooling and governance in AI-assisted development.</span><span class='zh'>AI 编码代理确实能产生更多代码，但由于质量、模块化和集成方面的挑战，未能带来更多可用软件。此现象强调在 AI 辅助开发中需要更好的工具链、标准与治理。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>9.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>218</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50059523' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>187</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50058528" data-id="50058528" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="270" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50058528" data-time="1791821590">
<a class="thumb" href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=prophitt.me&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>prophitt.me</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://prophitt.me/writing/a-modern-web-app-in-c" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Modern Web App in C</span><span class='zh'>用 C 构建现代 Web 应用</span></a><span class="dom">prophitt.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article argues that modern web apps can be built in C, likely via WebAssembly, highlighting architecture, tooling, and performance trade-offs. It challenges conventional language choices and invites reconsideration of full-stack approach.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何用 C 构建现代 Web 应用，可能通过 WebAssembly 实现前端与后端的高性能协作，分析架构、工具链与性能取舍。它挑战了传统的语言选择观，鼓励重新考虑全栈开发的路径。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>8.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>270</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50058528' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>111</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50057278" data-id="50057278" data-tags="Legal|Security|AI|Politics" data-score="297" data-comments="307" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278" data-time="1791816757">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#64748b' data-tag='Legal'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mortoray.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mortoray.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mortoray.com/why-arent-ai-companies-being-charged-for-hacking/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why aren&#x27;t AI companies being charged for hacking?</span><span class='zh'>为什么 AI 公司没有因为黑客行为被起诉？</span></a><span class="dom">mortoray.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that charging AI companies for hacking is structurally difficult: attribution, intent, and the role of autonomous agents muddle liability, and current laws don’t map cleanly onto AI-enabled wrongdoing. Until regulators clarify what counts as negligence or complicity, prosecutions against AI firms remain rare even when their tech is involved in breaches; the piece calls for clearer accountability frameworks to avoid chilling innovation.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，针对AI公司因黑客行为提起指控在法律上存在结构性困难：归责、意图与自动化代理的作用使责任难以界定，现行法难以直接套用到AI相关的违法行为。除非监管框架明确企业在何种情形承担共犯或疏忽责任，否则相关起诉往往难以推进。作者呼吁建立更清晰的问责规则，以避免对创新造成过度约束。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>6.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>297</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50057278' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>307</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-50054207" data-id="50054207" data-tags="Finance|Legal|Politics" data-score="573" data-comments="193" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50054207" data-time="1791800487">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/abc3/live/b06f9880-c621-11f1-bc2e-018d645d8d21.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='bbc.com' data-c='#84cc16' data-tag='Finance'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c64g1l5389kvo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Deloitte falsified documents to evade tax on Abramovich&#x27;s wealth</span><span class='zh'>德勤伪造文件以逃税阿布拉莫维奇财富</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations.</span><span class='zh'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Deloitte allegedly falsified documents to evade taxes on Roman Abramovich&#x27;s wealth, according to UK investigations. The revelation adds to the scrutiny around professional services firms and tax avoidance practices, with potential implications for audits, liability, and regulatory oversight.</span><span class='zh'>据英国调查称，德勤伪造文件以规避罗曼·阿布拉莫维奇财富的税务。这一披露凸显专业服务机构在税收规避中的争议，引发对审计责任、合规与监管监管的潜在影响的关注。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/qgis/QGIS-Enhancement-Proposals/pull/387" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>QGIS enacts moratorium on AI contributions</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 对 AI 贡献实施暂停</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>QGIS has enacted a moratorium on accepting AI-generated contributions while governance and licensing concerns are assessed. The move signals open-source communities grappling with risk and quality control as AI tooling becomes more integrated. It may slow AI-assisted features in the GIS project but could set precedent for other OSS projects.</span><span class='zh'>QGIS 已暂停接受基于 AI 的贡献，原因在于对许可证、质量和滥用风险的担忧。此举显示开源社区在 AI 工具日益整合的环境下面临的风险与质量控制挑战，可能放慢该 GIS 项目中 AI 助力的功能部署，但也可能为其他开源项目树立先例。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://uk.finance.yahoo.com/news/french-supermarket-chains-cyberattack-forced-115141288.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>French supermarkets suffer fridge shutdown after &#x27;unprecedented&#x27; cyberattack</span><span class='zh'>法国超市因‘前所未有’的网络攻击导致冷藏设备停摆</span></a><span class="dom">uk.finance.yahoo.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>French supermarkets faced fridge shutdowns due to an unprecedented cyberattack, disrupting refrigerated inventory and potentially pricing.</span><span class='zh'>法国多家超市因一场史无前例的网络攻击而遭遇冷藏设备停摆，影响冷藏库存以及潜在的价格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>French supermarkets faced fridge shutdowns due to an unprecedented cyberattack, disrupting refrigerated inventory and potentially pricing. It highlights retail IoT security vulnerabilities and supply chain risk.</span><span class='zh'>法国多家超市因一场史无前例的网络攻击而遭遇冷藏设备停摆，影响冷藏库存以及潜在的价格。此事件凸显零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性与供应链风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/cyber-security/unprecedented-cyberattack-cripples-supermarket-fridges-across-europe-as-hackers-activate-automatic-defrosting-delivery-and-store-prices-also-impacted-by-incident" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hackers activate automatic defrosting in supermarket fridges across Europe</span><span class='zh'>黑客在欧洲多国超市冷藏设备上启用自动除霜</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A coordinated cyberattack triggered automatic defrosting across supermarket fridges in Europe, disrupting storage conditions and potentially impacting pricing and stock. The incident underscores how IoT-enabled retail devices can be manipulated at scale, highlighting security gaps in supply chains and the importance of robust device hygiene.</span><span class='zh'>一次跨国网络攻击促使欧洲多家超市的冷藏设备启用自动除霜，干扰了冷藏条件，可能影响库存与价格。这一事件凸显了零售物联网设备的安全脆弱性，以及对供应链安全治理的迫切需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonfunctional.substack.com/p/passkeys-are-a-trap-do-not-use-them" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Passkeys are a trap</span><span class='zh'>Passkeys 是陷阱。请不要使用它们。</span></a><span class="dom">nonfunctional.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative stance arguing passkeys introduce risk, friction, or vendor lock-in rather than solving security problems. It cautions readers to consider trade-offs and to explore alternatives like hardware-backed keys or password managers. The piece invites debate about modern authentication design.</span><span class='zh'>这篇观点认为 Passkeys 并未真正解决安全问题，反而带来新的风险、使用摩擦和厂商锁定。强调权衡取舍并探讨硬件密钥、密码管理工具等替代方案，鼓励对现代认证设计的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Citroën revives the 2CV design with an electric powertrain, blending retro styling with modern EV tech.</span><span class='zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 的经典设计与电动动力系统相结合，展现了在可持续性背景下对传统车型的再造。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Citroën revives the 2CV design with an electric powertrain, blending retro styling with modern EV tech. The move showcases how nostalgic models can be reimagined for sustainability, while presenting challenges in preserving iconic character and efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>Citroën 将 2CV 的经典设计与电动动力系统相结合，展现了在可持续性背景下对传统车型的再造。此举既保留了怀旧风格，也带来在保持标志性特征与效率之间的平衡挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project shows turning a WiFi 7 router into an SDR receiver capable of 320 Msps, demonstrating repurposing consumer hardware for high-speed radio experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何将 WiFi 7 路由器改造成支持 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机，展示了用普通硬件进行高带宽无线信号采集的可行性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project shows turning a WiFi 7 router into an SDR receiver capable of 320 Msps, demonstrating repurposing consumer hardware for high-speed radio experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目展示如何将 WiFi 7 路由器改造成支持 320 Msps 的软件定义无线电接收机，展示了用普通硬件进行高带宽无线信号采集的可行性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://imgs.xkcd.com/comics/password_strength.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='royfish.dev' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://royfish.dev/fingerprints-are-the-new-cookies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Fingerprints are the new cookies</span><span class='zh'>指纹识别成为新的网络追踪手段</span></a><span class="dom">royfish.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Browser fingerprinting persists as a tracking technique despite cookie erosion, enabling unique combos of device attributes to identify users across sites. It underscores the ongoing tension between usability and privacy online.</span><span class='zh'>尽管 cookies 逐渐被边缘化，浏览器指纹仍作为一种持续的跟踪手段，通过设备属性的组合实现跨站点识别。文章讨论隐私影响及可能的对策，包括反指纹技术与浏览器保护措施，强调在线可用性与隐私之间的持续张力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>687</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50048642' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>329</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50047422" data-id="50047422" data-tags="AI" data-score="667" data-comments="974" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50047422" data-time="1791750052">
<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=twitter.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>twitter.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">15</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2109361546505966046" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The gap in shared understanding of LLM capability is widening</span><span class='zh'>对大型语言模型能力的共识差距正在扩大</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Different communities—researchers, engineers, and users—hold divergent expectations about what LLMs can do. The widening gap risks hype-driven deployments and misaligned policy. The piece advocates clearer benchmarks, transparent capability statements, and shared evaluation standards.</span><span class='zh'>研究者、工程师和用户对 LLM 能力的认知正在拉开差距，易导致被炒作驱动的部署与政策不匹配。文章主张建立更清晰的基准、公开的能力陈述和统一的评估标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/snuri00/ps2-web-recomp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PS2 games statically recompiled to WebAssembly</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：一个项目将 PS2 游戏静态重新编译为 WebAssembly，使其在浏览器中无需动态模拟就能运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: A project that statically recompiles PlayStation 2 games to WebAssembly, making them runnable in browsers without dynamic emulation layers. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://amosbbatto.wordpress.com/2026/10/05/linux-phones-alternative/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Will Linux phones become a viable alternative to the Android/iOS duopoly?</span><span class='zh'>Linux 手机会成为 Android/iOS 双头垄断的可行替代吗？</span></a><span class="dom">amosbbatto.wordpress.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece weighs hardware support, software ecosystems, and app availability as Linux phones mature. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://lalinsky.com/2026/10/11/type-safe-html-templating-in-zig.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Type-safe HTML templating in Zig</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现类型安全的 HTML 模板</span></a><span class="dom">lalinsky.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A type-safe HTML templating system is built in Zig using its compile-time features, providing strong guarantees against HTML injection and type errors.</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现了一套类型安全的 HTML 模板系统，利用编译时特性提供对 HTML 注入与类型错误的强保证。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A type-safe HTML templating system is built in Zig using its compile-time features, providing strong guarantees against HTML injection and type errors. The approach demonstrates how to implement templating that respects Zig&#x27;s safety while generating dynamic HTML, useful for embedded or systems projects that need UI.</span><span class='zh'>在 Zig 中实现了一套类型安全的 HTML 模板系统，利用编译时特性提供对 HTML 注入与类型错误的强保证。该方法展示如何在保持 Zig 安全性的同时生成动态 HTML，特别适合需要在嵌入式/系统项目中处理 UI 的场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://radar.cloudflare.com/bots/as13238?dateRange=7d#bot-traffic" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Yandex bot traffic down 99% after data center attack</span><span class='zh'>Yandex 爬虫流量在数据中心攻击后下降 99%</span></a><span class="dom">radar.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A data center attack cut Yandex bot traffic by about 99%, affecting automated indexing and monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>数据中心遭攻击后，Yandex 的爬虫流量下降约 99%，影响自动索引与监控服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A data center attack cut Yandex bot traffic by about 99%, affecting automated indexing and monitoring. The incident shows how infrastructure-level disruptions ripple into web services and analytics.</span><span class='zh'>数据中心遭攻击后，Yandex 的爬虫流量下降约 99%，影响自动索引与监控服务。此事件显示基础设施层面的中断如何波及到网页服务和分析工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://clickhouse.com/blog/posette-talk-recap-postgres-isnt-slow-your-storage-is" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Postgres isn&#x27;t slow, your storage is</span><span class='zh'>Postgres 不慢，存储才是瓶颈</span></a><span class="dom">clickhouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL performance is often limited by storage I/O rather than the database engine itself.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的性能经常被存储 I/O 所拖累，而非数据库本身。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PostgreSQL performance is often limited by storage I/O rather than the database engine itself. Optimizations should focus on storage subsystems—fast disks, SSDs, caching, and WAL throughput—to unlock real gains, with SQL tuning being secondary to I/O considerations.</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的性能经常被存储 I/O 所拖累，而非数据库本身。真正的提升往往来自优化存储子系统，如更快的磁盘、SSD、缓存和 WAL 吞吐量，而非单纯调优 SQL。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of selling vintage print issues of The Onion via an Archive Hunt-like platform, highlighting the quirks of media ephemera markets. It touches on archiving culture, copyright questions, and the tension between physical artifacts and digital availability.</span><span class='zh'>探索通过类似 Archive Hunt 的平台出售《The Onion》的印刷旧刊，揭示媒体浮游文献市场的怪异现象。涉及档案文化、版权问题，以及实体与数字可用性之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teorth.github.io/tao-web/slides/math-2-0-caltech-2026.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Terence Tao: Math 2.0 [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩：Math 2.0 [PDF]</span></a><span class="dom">teorth.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Terence Tao lays out a Math 2.0 vision in a Caltech talk — a framework blending computation, collaboration, and modern tooling to push mathematical progress. The slides outline practical implications for proof, education, and research infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>陶哲轩在卡内特加校的讲座中提出了‘Math 2.0’愿景，强调将计算、协作和新工具融入数学研究以推动进步。讲义暗示对证明方法、教育和研究基础设施的潜在影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A third Yandex Cloud data center was hit, highlighting ongoing fragility in regional cloud deployments. The incident tests customers&#x27; expectations for availability and the provider&#x27;s incident response and redundancy across sites. Implication: multi-region resilience and fast failover are essential for IaaS users.</span><span class='zh'>Yandex Cloud 的第三个数据中心遭遇故障，凸显区域性云部署的脆弱性。事件考验客户对可用性和云厂商跨区域冗余/应急响应的期望。意义在于强调对 IaaS 用户而言，多区域容错和快速故障转移至关重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>North Korea&#x27;s cartography reveals how the state wields geography as a tool of control.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦北朝鲜的地图制作，揭示国家如何通过地图来塑造边界与地名的叙事。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>North Korea&#x27;s cartography reveals how the state wields geography as a tool of control. The piece compares official maps with open data to highlight how borders and place names encode political messaging. It shows why map data matters for understanding information control and geopolitics.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦北朝鲜的地图制作，揭示国家如何通过地图来塑造边界与地名的叙事。将官方地图与开放数据的差异进行对比，体现地理信息背后的信息控制与隐性外交。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://insideclimatenews.org/news/07102026/exxonmobil-climate-deception-new-evidence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Documents add to evidence in climate deception case against ExxonMobil</span><span class='zh'>新证据充实埃克森美孚气候欺骗案</span></a><span class="dom">insideclimatenews.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New documents bolster evidence in the ExxonMobil climate deception case, potentially shaping the litigation and public accountability for fossil-fuel giants.</span><span class='zh'>新证据材料加强了埃克森美孚气候欺骗案的指控证据，可能影响诉讼走向与公众监督。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New documents bolster evidence in the ExxonMobil climate deception case, potentially shaping the litigation and public accountability for fossil-fuel giants. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nitter posted an Oct 10 update signaling fundraising and legal assistance to keep the project afloat amid potential legal pressure. Funding and legal support are central to its survival prospects.</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 在10月10日的一次更新中表示将寻求资金与法律援助，以应对潜在的法律压力而继续运营。此举凸显了面向隐私的开源社交前端在面对企业执法时的脆弱性。资金与法律支持成为其生存关键。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://shkspr.mobi/blog/2026/10/i-paid-people-to-try-and-follow-my-readme/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I paid people to try and follow my README</span><span class='zh'>我花钱让人尝试按我的 README 操作</span></a><span class="dom">shkspr.mobi</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test.</span><span class='zh'>作者花钱让人尝试按他的 README 做事，将文档变成一次真实测试。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author paid participants to try following his README, turning documentation into a live test. The results reveal ambiguities and blockers that real users encounter, highlighting gaps in how information is communicated. It offers practical lessons on writing clearer, testable onboarding docs.</span><span class='zh'>作者花钱让人尝试按他的 README 做事，将文档变成一次真实测试。结果暴露出真实用户在执行步骤时遇到的歧义和阻碍，凸显文档传达中的不足。为编写更清晰、可测试的上手文档提供了可操作的经验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ircv3.net/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>IRCv3</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 规范</span></a><span class="dom">ircv3.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IRCv3 defines modern extensions to the IRC protocol, enabling capability negotiation and richer metadata between clients and servers. It aims to make IRC more usable and interoperable in a world of feature-rich clients. The spec underpins improvements like extended joins and better identity handling.</span><span class='zh'>IRCv3 定义了对 IRC 协议的现代化扩展，支持客户端与服务器之间的能力协商与更丰富的元数据。它旨在让 IRC 在功能丰富的客户端时代更易用、互操作性更强。该规范支撑了扩展加入、身份处理等改进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.americanscientist.org/article/whatever-became-of-holography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Whatever became of holography? (2011)</span><span class='zh'>全息术到底发展到了哪儿？（2011）</span></a><span class="dom">americanscientist.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs.</span><span class='zh'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at holography as of 2011, tracing its promise in 3D imaging, data storage, and display against the realities of materials and manufacturing costs. It explains why practical, widespread holo-imaging remains elusive despite decades of research.</span><span class='zh'>回顾截至2011年的全息术发展，聚焦于3D成像、数据存储与显示的潜力与现实之间的矛盾。尽管研究持续，实际大规模应用仍受材料、制造成本与可扩展性等瓶颈制约。文章提醒读者，成像质量、规模化和成本是关键挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://housing.over.pizza/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A city-building game in which the city would prefer you didn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>一个城市似乎不想让你成功的城市建造游戏</span></a><span class="dom">housing.over.pizza</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a city-building game that foregrounds bureaucratic friction and civic pushback, where the city environment actively complicates development. Players must navigate red tape, budget constraints, and unpopular policies, turning urban planning into a tense strategy challenge.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一款强调官僚阻力与市政反对的城市建造游戏，城市环境会主动让开发变得复杂。玩家需应对繁文缭节的行政流程、预算约束和不受欢迎的政策，使城市规划成为紧张的策略对决。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nishtahir.com/build-your-own-decision-model/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Build your own decision model</span><span class='zh'>打造你自己的决策模型</span></a><span class="dom">nishtahir.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Offers a practical framework for building a personal decision model to navigate uncertainty, balancing probabilities, utilities, and decision rules.</span><span class='zh'>本文提供一个实用框架，帮助你在不确定情境中构建个人决策模型，结合概率、效用与决策规则。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Offers a practical framework for building a personal decision model to navigate uncertainty, balancing probabilities, utilities, and decision rules. The piece provides templates and steps to tailor the model across domains, helping maintain consistency in high-stakes choices.</span><span class='zh'>本文提供一个实用框架，帮助你在不确定情境中构建个人决策模型，结合概率、效用与决策规则。文中给出可定制的模板和步骤，便于在不同场景应用，提升关键决策的一致性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://disconnect3d.pl//2026/10/02/dont-put-tilde-in-your-path/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The tilde in your PATH may not be your HOME</span><span class='zh'>PATH中的~未必等同于HOME目录</span></a><span class="dom">disconnect3d.pl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Relying on tilde (~) in PATH can bite you because many shells don&#x27;t expand it in PATH entries.</span><span class='zh'>PATH中的~在许多外壳中不会在PATH条目里展开，可能导致目录解析出错。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Relying on tilde (~) in PATH can bite you because many shells don&#x27;t expand it in PATH entries. Use absolute paths or $HOME to avoid surprises.</span><span class='zh'>PATH中的~在许多外壳中不会在PATH条目里展开，可能导致目录解析出错。为避免意外，请改用绝对路径或$HOME来指定目录。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance.</span><span class='zh'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Berkeley study finds that even brief, 10-minute AI usage can erode persistence on hard tasks, prompting questions about cognitive effects of AI assistance. The finding has implications for education, productivity, and how tasks are structured in the age of AI.</span><span class='zh'>伯克利研究发现，即使仅使用 AI 十分钟也会削弱坚持艰难任务的能力，这对教育、生产力以及在 AI 时代如何设计任务具有重要意义。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.sciencealert.com/secret-of-orange-cats-finally-revealed-after-60-year-search" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/OrangeCatsSurprise.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sciencealert.com' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">50</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencealert.com/secret-of-orange-cats-finally-revealed-after-60-year-search" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Secret of Orange Cats Revealed After 60-Year Search</span><span class='zh'>橙色猫的秘密在60年的追寻后揭示</span></a><span class="dom">sciencealert.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A long-running genetic mystery about orange cats has finally been solved: orange fur is linked to a pigment gene on the X chromosome, meaning most orange cats are male and female orange cats are rarities caused by X-inactivation or unusual genetic events.</span><span class='zh'>关于橙色猫的长期遗传谜团终被揭晓：橙色毛色由位于X染色体上的色素基因控制，因此大多为公猫，雌性橙猫罕见，通常源于X染色体失活或异常基因事件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A long-running genetic mystery about orange cats has finally been solved: orange fur is linked to a pigment gene on the X chromosome, meaning most orange cats are male and female orange cats are rarities caused by X-inactivation or unusual genetic events. The finding clarifies how simple color genetics interact with sex chromosome biology and explains the observed distribution of orange cats in populations.</span><span class='zh'>关于橙色猫的长期遗传谜团终被揭晓：橙色毛色由位于X染色体上的色素基因控制，因此大多为公猫，雌性橙猫罕见，通常源于X染色体失活或异常基因事件。研究揭示了颜色遗传如何与性染色体生物学相互作用，以及橙猫在人群中的分布特征。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>198</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50037711' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>163</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-50049302" data-id="50049302" data-tags="Politics|Finance|Energy" data-score="31" data-comments="15" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50049302" data-time="1791762006">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/10/08/governments-are-mishandling-the-affordability-crisis" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f97316' data-tag='Politics'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=economist.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>economist.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">51</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/10/08/governments-are-mishandling-the-affordability-crisis" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Governments are mishandling the affordability crisis</span><span class='zh'>政府在可负担性危机上的应对失当</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Policy responses to rising living costs appear misaligned, with subsidies and price controls offering short-term relief but failing to tackle root drivers like productivity, housing supply, and energy dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>对上升的生活成本的宏观政策反应显现错配，补贴和价格管控只能提供短期缓解，未能触达生产率、住房供给与能源结构等根本驱动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Policy responses to rising living costs appear misaligned, with subsidies and price controls offering short-term relief but failing to tackle root drivers like productivity, housing supply, and energy dynamics. Without structural reforms, affordability pressures risk fueling slower growth and social discontent. The piece argues for more evidence-based, growth-oriented policies.</span><span class='zh'>对上升的生活成本的宏观政策反应显现错配，补贴和价格管控只能提供短期缓解，未能触达生产率、住房供给与能源结构等根本驱动。若不进行结构性改革，负担压力可能拖累增长并加剧社会不满。文章呼吁采用更具证据基础的、以提升生产力为目标的政策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://datacenters.google/locations/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Data Centers around the world</span><span class='zh'>谷歌全球数据中心分布</span></a><span class="dom">datacenters.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google operates a vast, world-spanning data-center network to support search, cloud services, and AI workloads, with attention to latency, redundancy, and energy sourcing. The data-centre footprint underpins modern internet services.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌运营着覆盖全球的数据中心网络，以支撑搜索、云服务和 AI 工作负载，强调低时延、冗余与能源采购。文章点出规模、区域性考量及可持续承诺和监管影响，强调数据中心是现代互联网服务的基础设施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pub.sakana.ai/mass/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Recursive self-improvement through collective intelligence</span><span class='zh'>通过集体智慧实现的递归自我提升</span></a><span class="dom">pub.sakana.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Examines how collective intelligence could fuel recursive self-improvement in AI systems, via feedback and collaboration among humans and agents. Iterative loops could push capabilities beyond a single-model ceiling, but governance and alignment become more complex at scale.</span><span class='zh'>研究通过集体智慧驱动 AI 的递归自我提升的可能性，借助人机与多代理的协作与反馈形成自我改进循环。随着协同放大能力，进展可能超出单一模型的上限，但规模化时在治理和对齐方面的挑战也随之增加。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ways-of-seeing.com/ch1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chapter 1 of Ways of Seeing by John Berger</span><span class='zh'>《看见的方式》第一章：约翰·伯格</span></a><span class="dom">ways-of-seeing.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>John Berger&#x27;s Ways of Seeing Chapter 1 challenges conventional looking by linking perception to social context and the male gaze.</span><span class='zh'>伯杰在第一章挑战了传统的“看”的观念，指出观感受社会语境和身份权力的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>John Berger&#x27;s Ways of Seeing Chapter 1 challenges conventional looking by linking perception to social context and the male gaze. It introduces how captions, framing, and the act of seeing shape meaning, not merely what is depicted. The foundation laid here continues to influence critical visual culture studies.</span><span class='zh'>伯杰在第一章挑战了传统的“看”的观念，指出观感受社会语境和身份权力的影响。图像的含义不仅来自画作本身，还由标题、分镜和叙述框架共同塑造。该章节奠定了对视觉文化的批评基础，至今影响设计与媒体研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://richwhitehouse.com/index.php?postid=78" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artificial Insanity</span><span class='zh'>人工疯狂</span></a><span class="dom">richwhitehouse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores the quirky, often surprising behavior of AI systems through the lens of &#x27;artificial insanity.&#x27; The piece calls out biases, hallucinations, and misalignment as systemic failure modes, arguing for humility about AI capabilities and better alignment practices.</span><span class='zh'>探讨人工智能系统的怪诞与不可预测行为，提出“人工疯狂”视角来审视偏见、幻觉与目标错配等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores the quirky, often surprising behavior of AI systems through the lens of &#x27;artificial insanity.&#x27; The piece calls out biases, hallucinations, and misalignment as systemic failure modes, arguing for humility about AI capabilities and better alignment practices.</span><span class='zh'>探讨人工智能系统的怪诞与不可预测行为，提出“人工疯狂”视角来审视偏见、幻觉与目标错配等问题。强调不要高估 AI 的可靠性，呼吁更强的对齐与鲁棒性研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://minesweeper.mikelacher.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Triple-A Minesweeper</span><span class='zh'>AAA级扫雷</span></a><span class="dom">minesweeper.mikelacher.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Triple-A Minesweeper showcases a highly polished, modern take on a classic puzzle, blending AAA production values with a timeless game.</span><span class='zh'>AAA级扫雷将经典益智游戏推向高水准的现代化呈现，拥有精致的美术、流畅的交互和性能优化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Triple-A Minesweeper showcases a highly polished, modern take on a classic puzzle, blending AAA production values with a timeless game. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rogerbinns/apsw" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>apsw: Another Python SQLite Wrapper</span><span class='zh'>apsw：又一个 Python SQLite 封装器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>APSW offers a Python SQLite wrapper with lower-level access and extra features beyond the standard interface. It exposes advanced APIs for virtual tables, custom collations, and direct blob handling, appealing to power users who need fine-grained control—though it requires more maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>APSW 是一个为 Python 提供 SQLite 封装的库，提供比内置 sqlite3 更低层次的访问和扩展功能。它支持高级 API，如虚拟表、定制排序以及直接操作 BLOB，适合需要更细粒度控制的高级开发者；不过使用起来需要更多手工维护和编码约束。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deno.com/blog/cloudflare" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare acquires Deno</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 收购 Deno，整合边缘计算生态</span></a><span class="dom">deno.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare is acquiring Deno, marrying Deno’s secure, TypeScript-friendly runtime with Cloudflare’s edge network and Workers platform. The deal could accelerate edge-based development and broaden Cloudflare’s developer toolkit, though it raises questions about governance and open-source leadership in the workspace.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 将收购 Deno，将 Deno 的安全、TypeScript 友好运行时与 Cloudflare 的边缘网络和 Workers 平台整合。此举可能加速基于边缘的开发并扩展 Cloudflare 的开发者工具包，同时也引发关于治理与开源领导力的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rea.tools/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>REA Reverse – Engineer Anything</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse：逆向工程万物</span></a><span class="dom">rea.tools</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>REA Reverse provides a hands-on platform for reverse engineering across domains, offering challenges and tutorials to dissect how software and hardware work. It aims to democratize RE skills by curating practical content and community-driven exercises. This matters for defenders, researchers, and hardware hackers looking to deepen code comprehension and vulnerability analysis.</span><span class='zh'>REA Reverse 提供一个聚焦逆向工程的实践平台，涵盖软件与硬件领域的挑战与教程，帮助用户拆解系统工作原理。通过实操内容和社区驱动的练习，推动逆向工程技能的普及。对安全研究、漏洞分析和硬件黑客都具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://community.bitwarden.com/t/published-version-update-in-app-stores/102750" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bitwarden Dual License Model</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 双许可证模式</span></a><span class="dom">community.bitwarden.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bitwarden introduces a dual-licensing model to balance openness with commercial viability, offering an AGPL-licensed community edition alongside a paid license for enterprise deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 推出双许可证模式，在社区版采用 AGPL 的同时提供企业级付费许可，以兼顾开源透明度与商业可行性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bitwarden introduces a dual-licensing model to balance openness with commercial viability, offering an AGPL-licensed community edition alongside a paid license for enterprise deployments. This approach aims to keep core software open while providing a compliant path for large organizations to deploy Bitwarden at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Bitwarden 推出双许可证模式，在社区版采用 AGPL 的同时提供企业级付费许可，以兼顾开源透明度与商业可行性。此举旨在为大型组织的部署提供合规路径，同时确保核心开源工作继续受益于社区贡献。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Attackers used the simple password 123456 to access Danish CPR records, exposing sensitive personal data.</span><span class='zh'>攻击者使用简单密码 123456 访问了丹麦 CPR 记录，暴露了敏感个人信息。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Attackers used the simple password 123456 to access Danish CPR records, exposing sensitive personal data. This incident shows how weak credentials can enable large scale government data breaches and underscores the need for stronger authentication and password hygiene across public-facing systems.</span><span class='zh'>攻击者使用简单密码 123456 访问了丹麦 CPR 记录，暴露了敏感个人信息。该事件凸显了弱密码如何使政府数据大规模泄露成为可能，也强调在面向公众的系统中需要更强的身份认证和密码管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2024-02-scandinavia-farmers-slaughtered-hunter-population.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scandinavia&#x27;s first farmers slaughtered hunter-gatherer population, DNA suggests</span><span class='zh'>DNA 显示斯堪的纳维亚首批农民屠杀猎人-采集者</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture.</span><span class='zh'>古代DNA研究显示，斯堪的纳维亚早期农民群体在向农耕社会过渡时，替代或暴力驱逐了猎人-采集者群体，为欧洲史前期人口动态的讨论提供新证据，涉及迁徙、文化与冲突。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ancient DNA suggests early farming populations replaced or violently displaced hunter-gatherers in Scandinavia, shedding light on population dynamics during Europe&#x27;s transition to agriculture. The finding informs debates on migration, culture, and conflict in prehistory.</span><span class='zh'>古代DNA研究显示，斯堪的纳维亚早期农民群体在向农耕社会过渡时，替代或暴力驱逐了猎人-采集者群体，为欧洲史前期人口动态的讨论提供新证据，涉及迁徙、文化与冲突。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://beaksec.github.io/posts/telegram-desktop-one-click-account-takeover/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop vulnerability allowed any user&#x27;s file to be stolen</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端漏洞致使任意用户文件可能被窃取</span></a><span class="dom">beaksec.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop suffered a vulnerability that let an attacker access and steal a victim&#x27;s files via the desktop client, potentially enabling a one-click account takeover.</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端存在漏洞，攻击者可通过桌面客户端访问并窃取受害者的文件，甚至实现一键式账户接管。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Telegram Desktop suffered a vulnerability that let an attacker access and steal a victim&#x27;s files via the desktop client, potentially enabling a one-click account takeover. The bug highlights how trusted desktop apps can become backdoors for data exfiltration, so users should update to patched versions and developers should harden local storage and permission handling.</span><span class='zh'>Telegram 桌面端存在漏洞，攻击者可通过桌面客户端访问并窃取受害者的文件，甚至实现一键式账户接管。这一漏洞凸显受信任的桌面应用可能成为数据外泄的后门，用户应尽快升级打补丁，开发者需加强本地存储与权限管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rociiu/talorys" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Talorys – A self-hosted personal AI agent on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier</span><span class='zh'>Talorys：在 Cloudflare 免费层自托管的个人 AI 代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talorys is a self-hosted personal AI assistant that runs on Cloudflare&#x27;s free tier, enabling edge-based AI without a traditional cloud subscription. It demonstrates how individuals can deploy private AI tools at the edge, balancing privacy, latency, and cost while avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>Talorys 是一个在 Cloudflare 免费层运行的自托管个人 AI 助手，支持在边缘执行 AI 而无需传统云订阅。它展示了个人在边缘部署私有 AI 工具的可能性，平衡隐私、低延迟和成本，同时降低对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://motherduck.com/blog/why-duckdb-20-is-faster/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why DuckDB 2.0 is faster</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 2.0 为什么更快</span></a><span class="dom">motherduck.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DuckDB 2.0 accelerates analytics workloads thanks to techniques like vectorized execution and query code generation, plus improved parallelism and memory management.</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 2.0 通过矢量化执行、查询生成代码、以及更高效的并行处理，显著提升分析查询的速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DuckDB 2.0 accelerates analytics workloads thanks to techniques like vectorized execution and query code generation, plus improved parallelism and memory management. The result is faster query times for typical analytic workloads, with architecture choices that favor throughput over raw CPU clocks.</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 2.0 通过矢量化执行、查询生成代码、以及更高效的并行处理，显著提升分析查询的速度。对分析型工作负载而言，这些架构优化带来更高吞吐和更低延迟。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.opengroup.org//openbrand/register/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple/macOS removed from official Unix registry</span><span class='zh'>苹果/macOS 已从官方 Unix 注册中移除</span></a><span class="dom">opengroup.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s macOS has been quietly removed from the Open Group&#x27;s official Unix branding registry, indicating a branding shift rather than a software change.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 macOS 已从开放组织的官方 Unix 品牌注册名单中移除，体现的是品牌层面的变化而非系统本身的改动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple&#x27;s macOS has been quietly removed from the Open Group&#x27;s official Unix branding registry, indicating a branding shift rather than a software change. The move matters for developers and vendors who rely on Unix branding for portability and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>苹果的 macOS 已从开放组织的官方 Unix 品牌注册名单中移除，体现的是品牌层面的变化而非系统本身的改动。此举可能影响那些依赖 Unix 品牌认知来展示兼容性和可移植性的开发者与厂商。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Donald Knuth once issued small reward checks for bug submissions, creating a legendary footnote in programming culture.</span><span class='zh'>唐纳德·克努斯曾以提交错误报告获得的小额奖励支票留下一段编程文化的传奇。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Donald Knuth once issued small reward checks for bug submissions, creating a legendary footnote in programming culture. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sustained RAM shortages are backstopping demand for DDR4 as new-generation modules lag supply.</span><span class='zh'>RAM短缺仍在推高DDR4需求，因为新一代内存供货滞后。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sustained RAM shortages are backstopping demand for DDR4 as new-generation modules lag supply. The shift affects hardware pricing, upgrade cycles, and AI workloads that still rely on DDR4 compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>RAM短缺仍在推高DDR4需求，因为新一代内存供货滞后。价格、升级周期以及仍依赖DDR4的AI工作负载都将因此受影响。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5vgjm3gk7rzo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/6fb8/live/8086eea0-c3cb-11f1-a64c-550be9e3c66b.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='bbc.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5vgjm3gk7rzo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is creating a &#x27;human premium&#x27; for art created by people</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在为人类创作的艺术带来“人类溢价”</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As AI-generated art becomes more capable, buyers are increasingly paying a premium for works created by humans, driven by notions of authenticity, process, and provenance.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 生成艺术品的能力提升，购买者越来越愿意为人类创作的作品支付“人类溢价”，原因在于对真实性、创作过程与来源的认同。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As AI-generated art becomes more capable, buyers are increasingly paying a premium for works created by humans, driven by notions of authenticity, process, and provenance. The trend could redraw pricing dynamics in the art market and incentivize artists to emphasize craft and storytelling. It also raises questions for platforms hosting both AI and human-made works.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 生成艺术品的能力提升，购买者越来越愿意为人类创作的作品支付“人类溢价”，原因在于对真实性、创作过程与来源的认同。这一趋势或将重塑艺术市场的定价格局，促使艺术家更加强调工艺与讲述能力，并对同时展出 AI 与人工创作作品的平台提出新问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.vt.edu/articles/2026/10/research_fralinbiomed_upfhutelin.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Food processing influences metabolism and brain activity</span><span class='zh'>食品加工影响代谢与脑活动</span></a><span class="dom">news.vt.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New research shows that how food is processed alters metabolism and brain activity, with implications for appetite control and nutrition policy.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明食品加工方式会影响代谢与大脑活动，对饮食调控、营养设计和公共卫生策略具有重要意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://robotgirlgang.com/2026/10/08/robot-is-a-social-construct-why-your-dishwasher-is-not-a-robot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>&quot;Robot&quot; Is a Social Construct: Why Your Dishwasher Is Not a Robot</span><span class='zh'>“机器人”是社会构造：为什么你的洗碗机不是机器人</span></a><span class="dom">robotgirlgang.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;robot&#x27; is a social construct framing how we classify machines, and that domestic devices like dishwashers lack autonomy and intent to be true robots.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为“机器人”是用来界定机器的社会建构，家用洗碗机等设备并不具备自主性与意图，因此并非真正意义上的机器人。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;robot&#x27; is a social construct framing how we classify machines, and that domestic devices like dishwashers lack autonomy and intent to be true robots. It invites readers to rethink labeling and consider actual capabilities versus marketing.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为“机器人”是用来界定机器的社会建构，家用洗碗机等设备并不具备自主性与意图，因此并非真正意义上的机器人。作者呼吁读者重新思考标签，并关注实际能力与营销之间的差异。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://patkerr.co.uk/2d-vehicles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>2D Vehicles</span><span class='zh'>2D 车辆系统</span></a><span class="dom">patkerr.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how to design and simulate vehicles in 2D, covering basic physics like mass, drag, wheels, and suspension, plus practical patterns for steering and traction. It emphasizes balancing realism with a lightweight engine, showing how small tweaks can dramatically affect handling.</span><span class='zh'>本文探讨如何在二维环境中设计与模拟车辆，涵盖质量、阻力、轮胎与悬架等物理基础，以及转向与牵引的实用实现方式。作者平衡真实感与简化，展示微小物理参数的调整如何影响轻量级引擎中的操控性与性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cockroachlabs.com/blog/experiment-running-hospital-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Five months treating bugs like patients and coding agents like a medical team</span><span class='zh'>把 Bug 当病人、把代码当医疗团队的五个月实验</span></a><span class="dom">cockroachlabs.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Shares a five-month experiment treating software bugs as patients and developers as a medical team, using triage, diagnostics, and postmortems to improve reliability.</span><span class='zh'>讲述五个月的实验，把故障视作病人、开发者当成医疗团队，采用分诊、诊断与事后分析来提升可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shares a five-month experiment treating software bugs as patients and developers as a medical team, using triage, diagnostics, and postmortems to improve reliability. The approach emphasizes collaborative debugging and structured incident response with measurable gains in system resilience.</span><span class='zh'>讲述五个月的实验，把故障视作病人、开发者当成医疗团队，采用分诊、诊断与事后分析来提升可靠性。此方法强调协同排错、结构化的事件响应，以及对系统健壮性带来的可量化提升。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An exploration of AI agents participating in stand-up meetings, delivering updates and aiding task planning, as a glimpse of human-agent collaboration in daily work.</span><span class='zh'>探讨 AI 代理在站立会议中的应用，负责汇报进展并协助任务规划，展示了日常工作中人机协作的前景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of AI agents participating in stand-up meetings, delivering updates and aiding task planning, as a glimpse of human-agent collaboration in daily work. It discusses how agents cope with sparse context and what benefits or drawbacks arise. Takeaway: AI assistants can augment team rituals, but need governance to stay helpful and not noisy.</span><span class='zh'>探讨 AI 代理在站立会议中的应用，负责汇报进展并协助任务规划，展示了日常工作中人机协作的前景。讨论代理在有限上下文下的表现及利弊。要点是 AI 助手可以提升团队例行流程，但需要适当的治理以避免产生干扰。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft Execution Containers 1.0.0 introduces policy-driven containment for AI agents, running them in isolated, auditable environments. This approach can help audit Rust projects and bridge interop gaps with C ecosystems, though it adds translation overhead and potential style differences.</span><span class='zh'>Eurydice 展示将 Rust 代码转换成可读的 C，便于调试和跨工具链移植。该方法有助于对 Rust 项目进行审核并促进与 C 生态的互操作，但会增加翻译开销与代码风格差异。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jessewaites.com/blog/post/i-pointed-ai-at-400-years-of-archives/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pointing AI at archives found a forgotten meteorite, lost rhinos, and more</span><span class='zh'>将 AI 应用于档案馆的检索：发现被遗忘的陨石、失踪的犀牛等</span></a><span class="dom">jessewaites.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI applied to centuries of archives surfaces discoveries like a forgotten meteorite and extinct rhinos, illustrating AI&#x27;s potential to aid historians and conservation.</span><span class='zh'>将 AI 应用于几十到上百年的档案检索，揭示了被遗忘的陨石和消失的犀牛等线索，显示 AI 在史料研究和保护工作中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI applied to centuries of archives surfaces discoveries like a forgotten meteorite and extinct rhinos, illustrating AI&#x27;s potential to aid historians and conservation. It demonstrates how search, OCR, and reasoning can unlock overlooked material.</span><span class='zh'>将 AI 应用于几十到上百年的档案检索，揭示了被遗忘的陨石和消失的犀牛等线索，显示 AI 在史料研究和保护工作中的潜力。该案例展示了文本检索、光学字符识别与推理等技术如何挖掘被长期忽视的材料。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post laments how rapid tooling, AI-generated outputs, and abstraction layers erode granular details we rely on to debug and reproduce systems.</span><span class='zh'>作者感叹快速工具、AI 生成输出和抽象层侵蚀了我们用于调试和复现系统的细节。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://epoch.ai/publications/innovationeval" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Recent AI models struggled to match a human algorithmic innovation</span><span class='zh'>近期 AI 模型难以赶上人类的算法创新</span></a><span class="dom">epoch.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent AI models still struggle to surpass human originality in algorithmic innovation, suggesting current benchmarks undervalue true novelty.</span><span class='zh'>最近的 AI 模型在算法创新的原创性方面仍难以超越人类。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent AI models still struggle to surpass human originality in algorithmic innovation, suggesting current benchmarks undervalue true novelty. The study argues that evaluating innovation requires new metrics and cross-domain tests to gauge genuine creative problem solving.</span><span class='zh'>最近的 AI 模型在算法创新的原创性方面仍难以超越人类。研究指出，现有基准难以衡量真正的创造力，需要新的评估方法来捕捉跨领域的创新能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A guide for Rust developers on C interop and FFI, covering how to call into C from Rust, share data safely, and common pitfalls when mixing languages.</span><span class='zh'>本文面向 Rust 开发者，讲解如何与 C 进行互操作（FFI），包括从 Rust 调用 C、数据共享的安全要点，以及混合语言时的常见坑。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rampart proposes browser-native on-device handling to minimize exposure of PII by processing data locally.</span><span class='zh'>Rampart 提出在浏览器本地处理数据、尽量减少个人信息暴露的方案，通过就地处理来降低向服务器传输敏感数据的需要，为设备端AI和隐私保护分析提供基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rampart proposes browser-native on-device handling to minimize exposure of PII by processing data locally. This approach reduces the need to trust remote servers for sensitive information and can be a building block for on-device AI and privacy-preserving analytics.</span><span class='zh'>Rampart 提出在浏览器本地处理数据、尽量减少个人信息暴露的方案，通过就地处理来降低向服务器传输敏感数据的需要，为设备端AI和隐私保护分析提供基础。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Old Macromedia Director games can be run on modern hardware using wrappers and emulation, with practical tips for preservation.</span><span class='zh'>老旧的 Macromedia Director 游戏可以通过封装与仿真等方法在现代硬件上运行，文中给出实用的保留技巧与步骤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Old Macromedia Director games can be run on modern hardware using wrappers and emulation, with practical tips for preservation. The post offers steps to keep classic titles playable.</span><span class='zh'>老旧的 Macromedia Director 游戏可以通过封装与仿真等方法在现代硬件上运行，文中给出实用的保留技巧与步骤。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An exploration of typical Git commit sizes and why smaller, purpose-driven commits improve code review and history tracing. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://shnatsel.github.io/safe-simd-in-rust-even-on-the-inside/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Safe SIMD in Rust, even on the inside</span><span class='zh'>Rust 中的安全 SIMD，即便在底层也安全</span></a><span class="dom">shnatsel.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how to use SIMD in Rust with safety guarantees, addressing unsafe blocks and presenting safer abstractions or crates that expose SIMD benefits without compromising memory safety. Highlights performance wins for numeric workloads and implications for libraries.</span><span class='zh'>探讨在 Rust 中实现带有安全保障的 SIMD，解决不安全代码的问题，并介绍更安全的抽象或库以在不牺牲内存安全的情况下获得 SIMD 性能。强调对数值工作负载的性能收益以及对库的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://phys.org/news/2026-10-chernobyl-particles-reveal-unexpectedly-stable.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Chernobyl particles reveal unexpectedly stable nuclear fuel after 40 years</span><span class='zh'>切尔诺贝利粒子揭示40年后核燃料的稳定性</span></a><span class="dom">phys.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers find that certain nuclear fuel particles from the Chernobyl fallout remain unexpectedly stable after four decades under intense radiation.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现，切尔诺贝利碎片中的某些核燃料粒子在强辐射下经过40年仍保持出人意料的稳定性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers find that certain nuclear fuel particles from the Chernobyl fallout remain unexpectedly stable after four decades under intense radiation. The finding informs long-term waste management, containment strategies, and material performance under extreme conditions.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现，切尔诺贝利碎片中的某些核燃料粒子在强辐射下经过40年仍保持出人意料的稳定性。这一发现为长期废物管理、密封与材料在极端条件下的性能提供新见解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openscad.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenSCAD the Programmers Solid 3D CAD Modeller</span><span class='zh'>OpenSCAD：程序员的3D CAD建模工具</span></a><span class="dom">openscad.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenSCAD is a script-based 3D CAD modeller favored by developers for parametric, reproducible designs.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSCAD 是一款以脚本驱动的3D CAD建模工具，面向开发者的参数化、可重复设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenSCAD is a script-based 3D CAD modeller favored by developers for parametric, reproducible designs. It emphasizes code over interactive GUI, enabling precise, repeatable models and easy versioning.</span><span class='zh'>OpenSCAD 是一款以脚本驱动的3D CAD建模工具，面向开发者的参数化、可重复设计。它强调以代码取代繁琐的图形界面，便于生成可重复、可版本化的模型。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-11111-4" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41586-026-11111-4/MediaObjects/41586_2026_11111_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='nature.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">113</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-11111-4" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Retrofitting language models to operate over bytes</span><span class='zh'>让语言模型直接按字节处理</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nature paper retrofits language models to operate over raw bytes instead of tokens, potentially improving multilingual robustness and robustness to text encoding.</span><span class='zh'>Nature 论文将语言模型改造为直接基于原始字节，而非分词后的标记，提升对多语言的鲁棒性与对文本编码的容忍性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nature paper retrofits language models to operate over raw bytes instead of tokens, potentially improving multilingual robustness and robustness to text encoding. The work discusses tradeoffs in model capacity and data efficiency and hints at rethinking tokenization. Takeaway: byte-level modeling could reshape how we handle text data in ML.</span><span class='zh'>Nature 论文将语言模型改造为直接基于原始字节，而非分词后的标记，提升对多语言的鲁棒性与对文本编码的容忍性。文中讨论了模型容量、数据效率等权衡，并提出对分词的重新思考。要点是字节级建模可能改变文本在机器学习中的处理方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Celld introduces deterministic simulation testing to ensure reproducible outcomes in simulations, enabling researchers to trust results across runs.</span><span class='zh'>Celld 提出确定性仿真测试，确保仿真结果在多次运行中的可重复性，从而提升研究可信度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Celld introduces deterministic simulation testing to ensure reproducible outcomes in simulations, enabling researchers to trust results across runs. The approach likely involves fixed seeds and recorded state to make experiments repeatable. Takeaway: determinism is essential for reliable AI/ML simulation studies.</span><span class='zh'>Celld 提出确定性仿真测试，确保仿真结果在多次运行中的可重复性，从而提升研究可信度。方法可能包括固定随机种子和记录状态以实现可重复性。要点是确定性对可靠的 AI/ML 仿真研究至关重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.datocms.com/blog/handling-less-common-scripts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Noto means &quot;no tofu&quot;: fixing dotted circles in Myanmar text</span><span class='zh'>Noto 意味着无豆腐块：修复缅甸文本中的点状圆圈</span></a><span class="dom">datocms.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s Noto font project aims to cover Myanmar script without rendering glitches like dotted circles, fixing rendering issues caused by diacritics and font shaping.</span><span class='zh'>Google 的 Noto 字体项目致力于让缅甸文等复杂脚本在数字环境中稳定显示，解决因变音符和字形连笔导致的点状圆圈等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s Noto font project aims to cover Myanmar script without rendering glitches like dotted circles, fixing rendering issues caused by diacritics and font shaping. The effort highlights how proper font support for complex scripts matters for accessibility and localization, even for languages with smaller digital footprints.</span><span class='zh'>Google 的 Noto 字体项目致力于让缅甸文等复杂脚本在数字环境中稳定显示，解决因变音符和字形连笔导致的点状圆圈等问题。对无障碍与本地化来说，良好的字体支持至关重要，即使对于使用者规模较小的语言也如此。</span></p>
